  (fea_conv): Conv2d(3, 112, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (post_unet_esa): ESA(
    (conv1): Conv2d(112, 28, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (conv2): Conv2d(28, 28, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
    (conv3): Conv2d(28, 28, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (conv_f): Conv2d(28, 28, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (conv4): Conv2d(28, 112, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
    (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
  )
  (post_unet_conv): Conv2d(112, 112, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (B1): P_HTCB(
    (tesa_in): TESA(
      (esa1): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(112, 28, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(28, 28, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(28, 28, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv_f): Conv2d(28, 28, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(28, 112, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa2): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(112, 28, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(28, 28, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(28, 28, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv_f): Conv2d(28, 28, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(28, 112, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa3): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(112, 28, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(28, 28, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(28, 28, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv_f): Conv2d(28, 28, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(28, 112, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
    )
    (tcn1): TCN(
      (omnisr): OmniSR(
        (patch_embed): Conv2d(112, 112, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (blocks): ModuleList(
          (0-1): 2 x OSAG(
            (local_dw): Conv2d(112, 112, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), groups=112, bias=False)
            (local_pw): Conv2d(112, 112, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
            (osa): OSA_Block(
              (norm1): LayerNorm((112,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
              (attn): WindowAttention(
                (qkv): Linear(in_features=112, out_features=336, bias=True)
                (attn_drop): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
                (proj): Linear(in_features=112, out_features=112, bias=True)
                (proj_drop): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
                (softmax): Softmax(dim=-1)
              )
              (drop_path): Identity()
              (norm2): LayerNorm((112,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
              (mlp): Sequential(
                (0): Linear(in_features=112, out_features=224, bias=True)
                (1): GELU(approximate='none')
                (2): Linear(in_features=224, out_features=112, bias=True)
                (3): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
              )
            )
          )
        )
        (norm): LayerNorm((112,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
      )
    )
    (tcn2): TCN(
      (omnisr): OmniSR(
        (patch_embed): Conv2d(112, 112, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (blocks): ModuleList(
          (0-1): 2 x OSAG(
            (local_dw): Conv2d(112, 112, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), groups=112, bias=False)
            (local_pw): Conv2d(112, 112, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
            (osa): OSA_Block(
              (norm1): LayerNorm((112,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
              (attn): WindowAttention(
                (qkv): Linear(in_features=112, out_features=336, bias=True)
                (attn_drop): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
                (proj): Linear(in_features=112, out_features=112, bias=True)
                (proj_drop): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
                (softmax): Softmax(dim=-1)
              )
              (drop_path): Identity()
              (norm2): LayerNorm((112,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
              (mlp): Sequential(
                (0): Linear(in_features=112, out_features=224, bias=True)
                (1): GELU(approximate='none')
                (2): Linear(in_features=224, out_features=112, bias=True)
                (3): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
              )
